Content: This IE indicates the Averaging Window for a QoS Flow.
Title Path: Elements for E1AP communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Maximum Data Burst Volume
Content: This IE indicates the Maximum Data Burst Volume for a QoS Flow and applies to delay critical GBR QoS flows only .
Title Path: Elements for E1AP communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Priority Level
Content: This IE indicates the Priority Level for a QoS Flow.
Title Path: Elements for E1AP communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Security Result
Content: This IE indicates whether the security policy indicated as "preferred" in the Security Indication IE is performed or not.
Title Path: Elements for E1AP communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Transaction ID
Content: The Transaction ID IE uniquely identifies a procedure among all ongoing parallel procedures of the same type initiated by the same protocol peer. Messages belonging to the same procedure shall use the same Transaction ID. The Transaction ID is determined by the initiating peer of a procedure.
Title Path: Elements for E1AP communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Paging Priority Indicator (PPI)
Content: The Paging Policy Indicator is used for paging policy differentiation (see details in TS 23.501 ).
Title Path: Elements for E1AP communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > gNB-CU-UP Capacity
Content: This IE indicates the relative processing capacity of an gNB-CU-UP with respect to other gNB-CU-UPs in order to load-balance among different gNB-CU-UPs.
Title Path: Elements for E1AP communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Maximum Integrity Protected Data Rate
Content: This IE indicates the maximum aggregate data rate for integrity protected DRBs for a UE as defined in TS 38.300 .
Title Path: Elements for E1AP communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > PDCP SN Status Information
Content: This IE contains information about PDCP PDU transfer status of a DRB.
Title Path: Elements for E1AP communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > QoS Flow Mapping List
Content: This IE contains a list of DRBs containing information about the mapped QoS flows.
Title Path: Elements for E1AP communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > QoS Flow Mapping Indication
Content: This IE is used to indicate whether only the uplink or only the downlink of a QoS flow is mapped to a DRB. For MBS this IE is applied to an MRB.
Title Path: Elements for E1AP communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Network Instance
Content: This IE provides the network instance to be used by the NG-RAN node when selecting a particular transport network resource as described in TS 23.501 .

Content: This IE provides information on the data usage.

Title Path: Elements for E1AP communication > Message and Information Element Abstract Syntax (with ASN.1) > General
Content: E1AP ASN.1 definition conforms to ITU-T Rec. X.691 , ITU-T Rec. X.680 and ITU-T Rec. X.681 . The ASN.1 definition specifies the structure and content of E1AP messages. E1AP messages can contain any IEs specified in the object set definitions for that message without the order or number of occurrence being restricted by ASN.1. However, for this version of the standard, a sending entity shall construct an E1AP message according to the PDU definitions module and with the following additional rules: IEs shall be ordered (in an IE container) in the order they appear in object set definitions. Object set definitions specify how many times IEs may appear. An IE shall appear exactly once if the presence field in an object has value "mandatory". An IE may appear at most once if the presence field in an object has value "optional" or "conditional". If in a tabular format there is multiplicity specified for an IE (i.e., an IE list) then in the corresponding ASN.1 definition the list definition is separated into two parts. The first part defines an IE container list where the list elements reside. The second part defines list elements. The IE container list appears as an IE of its own. For this version of the standard an IE container list may contain only one kind of list elements. NOTE: In the above ``IE'' means an IE in the object set with an explicit ID. If one IE needs to appear more than once in one object set, then the different occurrences will have different IE IDs. If an E1AP message that is not constructed as defined above is received, this shall be considered as Abstract Syntax Error, and the message shall be handled as defined for Abstract Syntax Error in clause 10.
Title Path: Elements for E1AP communication > Message and Information Element Abstract Syntax (with ASN.1) > Usage of private message mechanism for non-standard use
Content: The private message mechanism for non-standard use may be used: for special operator- (and/or vendor) specific features considered not to be part of the basic functionality, i.e., the functionality required for a complete and high-quality specification in order to guarantee multivendor interoperability; by vendors for research purposes, e.g., to implement and evaluate new algorithms/features before such features are proposed for standardisation. The private message mechanism shall not be used for basic functionality. Such functionality shall be standardised.

specified in ITU-T Recommendation X.691 . 9.6 Timers
Title Path: Handling of unknown, unforeseen and erroneous protocol data
Content: Section 10 of TS 38.413 is applicable for the purposes of the present document, with the following additions for non-UE-associated procedures: In case of Abstract Syntax Error, when reporting the Criticality Diagnostics IE for not comprehended IE/IEgroups or missing IE/IE groups, the Transaction ID IE shall also be included; In case of Logical Error, when reporting the Criticality Diagnostics IE, the Transaction ID IE shall also be included; In case of Logical Error in a response message of a Class 1 procedure, or failure to comprehend Transaction ID IE from a received message, the procedure shall be considered as unsuccessfully terminated or not terminated (e.g., transaction ID unknown in response message), and local error handling shall be initiated.
